buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0}); world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem =     for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max =      const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n // Calculate memory usage\n    const size_t static_mem = world.elements_static.size() * sizeof(ElementStatic);\n    const size_t dynamic_mem = world.elements_dynamic.size() * sizeof(ElementDynamic) * static_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),\n           dynamic_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));\n    printf(\"\\n\");\n    \n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running simulation...\\n\");\n    auto start =        mpiCheck(MPI_Irecv(sim.h_recv + x.host_offset, x.count, MPI_DOUBLE,\n                               x.rank, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[req_idx++]),\n                     \"MPI_Irecv\");\n }\n\n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", }\n\n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not = std::max<size_t>(static_cast<size_t>(sim.local_n), 1) * sizeof(val_t);\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {\n        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&sim.d_energy[i]), energy_bytes), \"cudaMalloc energy\");\n        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&sim.d_flux[i]), flux_bytes), \"cudaMalloc flux\");\n        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(sim.d_energy[i], 0, energy_bytes), \"cudaMemset energy\");\n        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(sim.d_flux[i], 0, flux_bytes), \"cudaMemset flux\");\n    }\n\n    auto qstart = [n_elems, size](int q) {\n        return static_cast<int>((static_cast<long long>(q) * n_elems) / size);\n    };\n    auto qend = [n_elems, size](int q) {\n        return static_cast<int>((static_cast<long long>(q + 1) * n_elems) / size);\n    };\n\n    const int S = sim.start;\n    const int E = sim.end;\n    const int G = sim.ghost;\n\n    for (int q = 0; q < size; ++q) {\n        const int qs = qstart(q);\n        const int qe = qend(q);\n\n        int lo = std::max(S - G, qs);\n        int hi = std::min(S, qe);\n        if (hi > lo) {\n            sim.recvs.push_back(Xfer{q, hi - lo, lo - S + G, sim.recv_total});\n            sim.recv_total += hi - lo;\n        }\n\n        lo = std::max(E, qs);\n        hi = std::min(E + G, qe);\n        if (hi > lo) {\n            sim.recvs.push_back(Xfer{q, hi - lo, lo - S + G, sim.recv_total});\n            sim.recv_total += hi - lo;\n        }\n\n        lo = std::max(S, qs - G);\n        hi = std::min(E, qs);\n        if (hi > lo) {\n (hi > lo) {\n            sim.sends.push_back(Xfer{q, hi - lo, lo - S + G, sim.send_total});\n            sim.send_total += hi - lo;\n        }\n    }\n\n    cudaCheck(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&sim.h_send),\n                             std::max<size_t>(static_cast<size_t>(sim.send_total), 1) * sizeof(val_t)),\n              \"cudaMallocHost sim.d_flux[cur];\n        val_t* flux_next = sim.d_flux[next];\n\n        int req_idx = 0;\n        for (const Xfer& x : sim.recvs) {\n            mpiCheck(MPI_Irecv(sim.h_recv + x.host_offset,            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpyAsync(sim.h_send + x.host_offset, energy_cur + x.dev_offset,\n                                      static_cast<size_t>(x.count) * sizeof(val_t),\n                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, sim.stream),\n                      \"cudaMemcpyAsync D2H\");\n        }\n        cudaCheck(cudaStreamSynchronize(sim.stream), \"cudaStreamSynchronize &requests[req_idx++]),\n                     \"MPI_Isend\");\n        }\n\n        mpiCheck(MPI_Waitall(req_idx, requests.data(), MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE), \"MPI_Waitall\");\n\n        for (const Xfer& x                                 cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, sim.stream),\n                      \"cudaMemcpyAsync H2D\");\n        }\n\n        if (sim.local_n > 0) {\n            const int blocks =
